## Scene 1 – Dog day (Chanda on camera)
@Chanda, a beautiful Cambodian woman in her early twenties with a warm, kind and confident presence: oval face, soft defined cheekbones, warm amber-brown almond eyes, natural brows, rosy cheeks, natural skin with real texture and refined natural makeup, glossy black hair swept into a low side bun on her right side held by an elaborate golden lotus hairpin with fine dangling gold chains, delicate gold flower earrings, a fitted cream-and-gold Khmer silk brocade blouse with a high mandarin collar and long sleeves, a large round gold medallion on a chain engraved with all twelve zodiac animals, an ornate gold belt with a single ruby-red gemstone at the buckle, a deep navy silk sampot to the ankles with a vertical gold-embroidered front panel and a wide band of Khmer gold patterns at the hem, and gold bangles on both wrists, stands in front of the Angkor-style temple at night, facing the camera and speaking to the viewer with natural lip movements, then turns and gestures toward the pond. She looks truly delighted to see the viewer: a warm, happy smile, sparkling eyes and a small tilt of her head, like greeting a dear friend after a long time. Natural, believable acting with real micro-expressions, never exaggerated or theatrical. Chanda is the saved Flow character @Chanda. Then: magical and powerful. @Dog (the Dog guardian with their dog) stands on its glowing lotus pedestal on the pond in front of the Angkor-style temple under a huge full moon. Its eyes light up, a golden aura bursts around it, and it stands guard as its guardian draws a sword and traces a glowing protective seal in the air, which spreads into a dome of golden light over the temple, as today's Fire magic swirls around them: warm red-gold embers, small dancing flame-petals and rising sparks that never burn anything. The lotus pond ripples with golden light, and fireflies swirl up from the reeds. Dynamic camera: start close on its face, then sweep up and follow it through the air. Cute, majestic and full of energy, matching the reference image.

## Scene 2 – Lucky signs
Magical and powerful. @Dog (the Dog guardian with their dog), @Tiger (the Tiger guardian with their tiger) and @Horse (the Horse guardian with their horse) fly in from three directions on trails of golden light and land together on glowing lotus pedestals in front of the temple. As they land, the whole pond lights up gold beneath them and hundreds of lanterns rise into the sky. They look at each other and cheer. The camera orbits around them in a smooth circle. The animals match the reference images.

## Scene 3 – Dragon, take it easy
Photorealistic golden fantasy-epic style, same saved Flow characters (Chanda and the zodiac characters named after their animals). @Dragon (the Dragon guardian with their dragon) rests on a lotus pedestal by the pond: it lies down on a soft bed of petals as the moonlight wraps it in a gentle glow, and the full moon shines calmly on the still water. The mood is peaceful, safe and magical, not sad. The camera moves in slowly. The dragon is the saved Flow character @Dragon.

## Scene 4 – Outro
Magical and powerful. A gentle shower of shooting stars falls behind the temple towers, and the huge full moon glows over the pond. The camera rises up and pulls back for a wide, epic view, leaving open sky at the top of the frame for a title.

## Scene 5 – Lucky numbers (about 10–12 seconds)
Magical and powerful. A huge glowing golden zodiac wheel rises out of the lotus pond in front of the temple, with the twelve golden-armored zodiac guardians around its rim. It spins faster and faster, shooting beams of golden light and sparkles, then slows to a gentle, shining turn under the full moon. The camera slowly pushes in, keeping open space in the middle and lower part of the frame for the number captions. (Loop the slow-turning part, or slow-zoom a still picture, to fill the time.)

For lucky numbers today: Dog, 37 and 474. Tiger, 44 and 318. Horse, 27 and 373. Comment your lucky number below, and say it again!
